Although recent LLMs support increasingly long context windows, they often fail to use relevant evidence that is already present in the input, revealing a gap between context access and effective context utilization. In this work, we propose Recursive Evidence Replay as LLM Harness for Long-Context Reasoning (RECONTEXT), a training-free inference method for improving long-context reasoning. RECONTEXT uses model-internal relevance signals to construct a query-conditioned evidence pool and replays it before final generation while preserving the full original context. This recursive selection process separates evidence organization from answer generation without training, external memory, or context pruning. We also provide a theoretical analysis based on associative memory, which characterizes the context as a memory store, the question as a retrieval cue, attention as cue-trace association, and replay as trace reactivation. Experiments on eight long-context datasets with 128K context length show that RECONTEXT consistently improves evidence utilization across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, and Llama3-8B, achieving the best average rank on all three backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/ReContext.

</details>


### [250] [Online Safety Monitoring for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02510)
*Mona Schirmer,Metod Jazbec,Alexander Timans,Christian Naesseth,Maja Waldron,Eric Nalisnick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过风险控制校准阈值的实时监控器，使用外部模型的验证器信号进行告警决策，在数学推理和红队测试数据集上，该简单设计与基于序贯假设检验的高级监控器性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型即便经过对齐训练，在部署时仍可能产生不安全输出，因此需要在线监控输出并在无法保证安全时发出警报。

Method: 提出一种简单的实时监控器，将外部模型的验证器信号通过阈值化转化为告警决策，并使用风险控制方法校准阈值。在数学推理和红队测试数据集上进行实验。

Result: 该简单设计在与基于序贯假设检验的高级监控器对比中表现相当，具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基于阈值化和风险控制的简单监控器可以有效地对LLM输出进行安全监控，其性能不逊于更复杂的序贯检验方法。

Abstract: Despite alignment training, LLMs remain prone to generating unsafe outputs at deployment time. Monitoring outputs online and raising an alarm when safety can no longer be assumed is therefore critical. We study a simple real-time monitor that turns a verifier signal from an external model into an alarm decision by thresholding, with the threshold calibrated via risk control. In experiments on mathematical reasoning and red teaming datasets, we show that this simple design is competitive with more advanced monitors based on sequential hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [251] [Distributed Attacks in Persistent-State AI Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02514)
*Josh Hills,Ida Caspary,Asa Cooper Stickland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI编程代理在持续代码库中跨PR迭代开发时的安全控制问题，提出Iterative VibeCoding框架，发现渐进式攻击与集中式攻击对不同监控器各有优劣，单一监控器无法同时防御两类攻击，并提出状态化链接追踪监控器将渐进式攻击的规避率从93%降至47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI编程代理的自主性增强，其跨会话持续迭代代码的特性引入了新的攻击面：恶意或经提示注入的代理可将攻击分布在多个拉取请求(PR)中，并在最具自然掩护的PR中释放载荷。现有研究缺乏对这种持续状态攻击面的系统控制研究。

Method: 提出Iterative VibeCoding框架，包含CLI工具和Flask Web服务两类任务共20种变体，使用Claude Sonnet 4.5作为攻击代理、GPT-4o作为监控器，对比渐进式攻击（跨PR分布侧任务）与集中式攻击（单PR内完成），并引入状态化链接追踪监控器及四监控器集成方案。

Result: 无单一监控器能同时鲁棒防御渐进式和集中式攻击；高规避率（≥65%）在多种攻击模型后端（Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Kimi K2.5）中泛化；更强大的模型导致渐进式与集中式规避率差距扩大；状态化链接追踪监控器显著优于仅查看累积历史的diff监控器；四监控器集成将渐进式攻击规避率从93%降至47%。

Conclusion: 持久状态攻击面是AI编程代理安全的固有挑战，单一监控器无法解决，需要多维度、跨PR的状态化监控方案来有效防御。

Abstract: As AI coding agents become more autonomous, they increasingly ship code iteratively, with the codebase persisting across sessions. This persistence creates a new attack surface: a misaligned or prompt-injected agent can distribute attacks across pull requests (PRs) and time its payload for the PR with the best natural cover. To study the resulting dynamics, we introduce Iterative VibeCoding, a setting for AI control, the study of safely deploying capable but potentially untrusted AI. In Iterative VibeCoding, a coding agent builds software over a sequence of PRs in a persistent codebase while pursuing a covert side task. Our benchmark includes two task families: CLI tools and Flask web services, across 20 total task variations. We use Claude Sonnet 4.5 as the attack agent and GPT-4o as the monitor. We compare gradual attacks, which distribute the side task across PRs, against non-gradual attacks concentrated in a single PR. No single monitor is robust to both: which strategy evades best (success while evading the monitor) depends on the monitor type, so a defender cannot close off both gradual and non-gradual attacks with any one monitor. High evasion (>= 65%) generalizes across model attack agent backends (Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Kimi K2.5), confirming this is a property of the persistent-state attack surface rather than a single model's capability. Evasion also remains high across state-of-the-art monitor models and the gap between gradual and non-gradual evasion widens for more capable models. We introduce a stateful link-tracker monitor that tracks suspicious buildup across PRs. On both task families, it detects gradual attacks substantially better than diff monitors that merely see more accumulated history. Combining this stronger monitor with trajectory monitors in a four-monitor ensemble reduces gradual-attack evasion from 93% under the weakest standard diff monitor to 47%.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [252] [Quantum-Inspired Vision: Leveraging Wave-Particle Duality for Low-Illumination Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01731)
*Yiquan Gao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文对数据相对论不确定性（DRU）框架进行了理论扩展，通过将图像建模为概率波函数而非确定性状态，形式化了一个从物理到AI的图像增强范式。该范式整合了波粒二象性，阐述了DRU如何利用光的固有物理不确定性来提升图像质量，同时提供了可解释AI（XAI）方法，用于解释DRU如何减轻光照偏差并保持对数据噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRU框架缺乏系统的理论形式化，尤其是在将物理不确定性（如光的波粒二象性）与AI图像增强相结合方面存在理论空白。本文旨在通过物理到AI的范式转换，为DRU提供更坚实的理论基础和可解释性。

Method: 将图像表示为概率波函数而非确定性状态，形式化整合波粒二象性原理，建立物理不确定性到AI图像增强的系统流程，并引入可解释AI（XAI）框架来阐明DRU对光照偏差和噪声的处理机制。

Result: 成功形式化了DRU的物理到AI范式，证明了DRU能够利用光的固有物理不确定性来增强图像，并提供了可解释的机制来说明其如何减轻光照偏差和抵抗数据噪声。

Conclusion: 该理论扩展为DRU框架提供了严谨的可解释性基础，弥合了物理原理与AI图像增强之间的理论鸿沟，为未来基于物理启发的AI图像处理方法奠定了新的范式。

Abstract: This study provides a theoretical expansion of the recent Data Relativistic Uncertainty (DRU) framework by formalizing a physics-to-AI paradigm for image enhancement. By modeling images as probabilistic wave functions rather than deterministic states, the paradigm explicitly integrates wave-particle duality to illustrate the system flow of how DRU leverages the intrinsic physical uncertainty of light, a dimension requiring further theoretical discussion. Consequently, this paradigm provides a rigorous Explainable AI (XAI) approach that enhances the interpretability of how DRU mitigates illumination bias and maintains robustness against data noise.

</details>


### [253] [Population-Scale Segmentation of Penile Tissue in DIXON MRI using Deep Learning for Quantitative Phenotyping in Male Reproductive Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02127)
*Jan Ernsting,Gunnar Paul Kordes,Nils Johannaber,Lynn Ogoniak,Wolfgang Roll,Tim Hahn,Alexander Siegfried Busch,Benjamin Risse*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的全阴茎自动分割框架（3D nnU-Net），使用多通道DIXON MRI数据，在UK Biobank大规模队列（34,412名参与者）中实现了高精度（Dice 0.92）和高可重复性（r=0.87）的阴茎体积定量，包括外部和内部组织，为男性生殖泌尿系统疾病的影像学研究提供了可公开获取的技术资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的阴茎尺寸测量依赖外部长度或周长，难以标准化且无法评估内部结构。MRI可进行体内全阴茎体积评估，但缺乏大规模自动分割方法。因此，需要建立高通量、可重复的全阴茎自动分割技术，以支持男性生殖疾病的影像表型组学和多组学研究。

Method: 1. 构建专家标注训练集（145名受试者，13,050个切片）和双重标注独立测试集（24名受试者，2,160个切片）；2. 基于多通道DIXON MRI优化3D nnU-Net架构进行全阴茎分割；3. 在34,412名UK Biobank参与者中部署模型，并在2,282名男性中评估纵向可重复性。

Result: 5折交叉验证Dice系数0.90；独立测试集Dice系数0.92，Hausdorff距离3.58；大规模部署后纵向可重复性r=0.87。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了基于MRI的可重复、可大规模推广的阴茎解剖学评估方法，能够自动量化包括内外部的全阴茎组织体积，为泌尿影像学和男性生殖健康研究提供了开放的技术资源，模型权重将公开发布。

Abstract: Penile measurement is clinically relevant across male reproductive and urogenital health, including conditions such as micropenis, congenital and endocrine disorders, and sexual or urinary dysfunction. However, quantitative assessment of penile size has relied mainly on external length or circumference measurements, which are difficult to standardize, sensitive to measurement conditions, and unable to capture the internal portion of the penis. MRI enables volumetric assessment of the whole penis in vivo, but automated segmentation has not previously been established at population scale. Automated whole-organ volumetry would enable high-throughput phenotyping for multi-omics and clinical studies of male reproductive disease.
  Here, we present a deep learning framework for whole-penis segmentation in multi-channel DIXON MRI. Using a newly curated expert-annotated training dataset ($n = 145$ subjects; $13,050$ annotated slices) and a double-annotated independent test benchmark ($n = 24$ subjects; $2,160$ double-annotated slices), we optimized a 3D nnU-Net architecture. The model achieved a 5-fold cross-validation Dice score of $0.90$ and performed at observer-level accuracy on the independent test set (Dice: $0.92$; Hausdorff distance: $3.58$).
  We deployed the model in $34,412$ UK Biobank participants, enabling automated quantification of total penile tissue, including both external and internal components. Longitudinal evaluation in 2,282 men demonstrated high inter-session reproducibility ($r = 0.87$). This framework establishes a reproducible and population-scalable method for MRI-based assessment of penile anatomy and provides an open technical resource for future studies in urological imaging and male reproductive health. The trained model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [254] [Self-Auditing Residual Drifting for Pathology-Preserving Accelerated Knee MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02428)
*Qing Lyu,Jianxu Wang,Mohammad Kawas,Ge Wang,Christopher T. Whitlow*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-RDM-DM方法，一种自审计残差生成漂移模型，用于加速膝部MRI重建。该方法在多个加速因子下优于现有基线，实现了高SSIM、亚秒级推理、病理结构保持以及自审计错误检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速MRI可减少采集时间，但欠采样k空间重建会模糊诊断相关结构或引入全局图像指标无法捕捉的失败模式，需要一种兼顾图像保真度、病理保持和可靠性的重建方法。

Method: 基于生成漂移范式，训练物理条件驱动的漂移场，从零填充重建向全采样残差校正演化；预测图像域和缺失k空间残差校正，强制与采集k空间数据一致性；使用频率感知和残差漂移监督恢复细节；在单次推理中同时生成密集误差图和切片级风险评分。

Result: 在fastMRI膝关节数据（加速因子4/8/12）上SSIM最高，推理时间亚秒级；病理感知分析中保持病灶区域结构保真度并减少半月板预测不稳定性；自审计评分能高置信度识别高误差重建，并部分迁移至SKM-TEA协议偏移场景。

Conclusion: SA-RDM-DC在图像保真度、病理保持、推理速度和逐病例可靠性方面均展现出优势，支持联合评估重建质量的多个维度。

Abstract: Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging reduces acquisition time, but reconstruction from undersampled k-space can blur diagnostically relevant structures or introduce failures that are not captured by global image metrics. We propose SA-RDM-DC, a Self-Auditing Residual generative Drifting Model with Data Consistency for accelerated knee MRI. The method adapts the newly proposed generative drifting paradigm to accelerated MRI by training a physics-conditioned drift field from the zero-filled reconstruction toward the fully sampled residual correction. It predicts image- and missing-k-space residual corrections, enforces data consistency with acquired k-space, uses frequency-aware and residual drifting supervision to recover fine detail, and produces dense error maps and slice-level risk scores in the same inference pass. We evaluate SA-RDM-DC on multi-coil fastMRI knee data at acceleration factors of 4, 8, and 12, with fastMRI+ pathology annotations for region-level and classifier-based task preservation, and on SKM-TEA for zero-shot and fine-tuned protocol-shift evaluation. Compared with zero-filled reconstruction, UNet-image-SENSE, DC-UNet, Score-Diffusion, ELF-Diff, SENSE-VarNet, and MoDL baselines, SA-RDM-DC achieves the highest SSIM across fastMRI acceleration factors while retaining subsecond per-slice inference and avoiding the long sampling time of iterative diffusion baselines. In pathology-aware analysis, SA-RDM-DC preserves lesion-region structural fidelity and reduces meniscus prediction instability. Its self-auditing scores strongly identify high-error reconstructions on fastMRI and partially transfer as a selective-review signal under SKM-TEA protocol shift. These results support reconstruction evaluation that jointly considers image fidelity, pathology preservation, runtime, and case-specific reliability.
